SwitchYard - fixed-commit upstream NuGet license evidence
Snapshot collected 2026-10-11 from the nuspec repository commit, or an official exact-version release tag resolved to a commit.
This is a manually maintained supplement to nuget-inventory.json and nuget-NOTICES.txt.
The offline collector does not fetch, regenerate, or overwrite this supplement.

Scope: repository-root LICENSE/COPYING/NOTICE files at exact commits. Version-tag mappings are identified in the source register.
A version tag is upstream release evidence; it does not claim that an omitted nuspec commit was present.
Text is copied without changing its terms; line endings normalized. SHA-256 hashes refer to raw fetched bytes.
This does not prove that every nested, embedded, generated, or native dependency is covered.
In particular OR-Tools native libraries and SQLite native binaries need component-level release review.
Top-level license retrieval is not a certification of redistribution readiness.

Local package versions initially missing standalone text: 70
Package versions with fixed-commit upstream root text: 70
Unique fetched texts: 28

UNRESOLVED UPSTREAM ROOT TEXTS

None within the root-text scope above. Embedded/native component review remains open.
